# The Immigration and Nationality (Fees) Regulations 2018

> Text as at 2026-11-02.
>
> Extent: England, Wales, Scotland, Northern Ireland.

*Extent: E+W+S+N.I.*

2018 No. 330

Immigration

Nationality

The Immigration and Nationality (Fees) Regulations 2018

Made

15th March 2018

Laid before Parliament

16th March 2018

Coming into force in accordance with regulation 1(2)

The Secretary of State makes the following Regulations with the consent of the Treasury, in exercise of the powers conferred by sections 68(1), (7), (8) and (10), 69(2), and 74(8)(a), (b) and (d) of the Immigration Act 2014 [^c22924881]. These Regulations are made further to provision in the Immigration and Nationality (Fees) Order 2016 [^c22924891].

## 1. Citation, commencement and extent

(1) These Regulations may be cited as the Immigration and Nationality (Fees) Regulations 2018.

(2) These Regulations come into force on 6th April 2018.

(3) Subject to paragraphs (4) to (6), these Regulations extend to England and Wales, Scotland and Northern Ireland.

(4) This regulation and regulations 2, 11, 13A,[^c-f233ec1fe82df000b12984ce08209b24] 13B, 13C, 13D,[^c-2490cd8644db6e3ebfa7fa92bcfaca6e] 15 and 17 and Schedule 9 also extend to the Isle of Man, but only for the purpose of issuing entry clearance to enter the Isle of Man.

(5) This regulation and regulations 2, 12, 13A,[^c-a195e5bbc5844cd2b7f9362b6201b5c9] 13B, 13C, 13D,[^c-d9fe83f6e3f686e68c3e161a83482b2e] 15 and 17 and Schedule 10 also extend to the Bailiwick of Guernsey and the Bailiwick of Jersey, but only for the purpose of issuing entry clearance to enter the bailiwick concerned.

(6) Paragraphs (a) and (b) of regulation 18 have the same extent as the instruments which they revoke.

## 2. Interpretation

- “administrative review” means administrative review under Appendix[^c-627e22e9fea5feef72b25a4c52a4552f] Administrative Review[^c-5ef332d8a747af78687bbaab0763236f] to the immigration rules or under Part 5 of the Citizens’ Rights (Frontier Workers) (EU Exit) Regulations 2020[^c-5e1454e710ba72da33bd58e0566fd4a5];

  - (a) entry clearance to enter or leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Global Talent, Appendix Innovator, Appendix Innovator Founder or Appendix Start-up to those rules;; or

  - (a) section 22(1) of the Children Act 1989 [^c22924991];
  - (b) section 17(6) of the Children (Scotland) Act 1995 [^c22925001];

- “**biometric immigration document**” has the same meaning as provided in section 5 of the 2007 Act [^c22925031];

- “**contractor**” means a person with whom the Secretary of State has entered into a contract, by which the person agrees to provide certain services in connection with immigration or nationality to applicants;
- “**Convention rights**” means the rights identified as Convention rights by section 1 of the Human Rights Act 1998 [^c22925051];
- “dependant” in respect of a person (“P”) means—

(2)[^c-30ab39e3bb19316a729f81ea459d35a3] For the purposes of these Regulations, a person is “in the Family Returns Process” if they fall within the meaning of that term in paragraph 6 of the immigration rules.

## 3. Fees for applications, processes and services in connection with immigration and nationality

Schedule 1 (applications for entry clearance to enter, and leave to enter, the United Kingdom) has effect to specify—

- (a) the amount of the fees for—
  - (i) specified applications for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom for the purposes of article 4 of the 2016 Order;
  - (ii) specified applications for entry clearance to enter and leave to enter the United Kingdom for the purposes of article 5 of the 2016 Order;
  - (iii) specified applications for [^c-aa6a86852526a721c0cce0241fec0988]... an approval letter from an endorsing body[^c-1c42f3f3ca66b931ce442879b1a4406a] for the purposes of article 6 of the 2016 Order; and
- (b) exceptions to the requirement to pay fees referred to in paragraph (a), and [^c-521c215420c184e6162abee6d8261e76]... circumstances in which such fees may be waived or reduced.

## 4.

Schedule 2 (applications for leave to remain in the United Kingdom) has effect to specify—

- (a) the amount of the fees for—
  - (i) specified applications for leave to remain in the United Kingdom for the purposes of articles 4 and 5 of the 2016 Order;
  - (ii) applications for an approval letter from an endorsing body[^c-daa053801541859ecc2848b2bb1c8851] for the purposes of article 6 of the 2016 Order; and
- (b) exceptions to the requirement to pay fees referred to in paragraph (a), and [^c-9a1693166f33ca6fd047aa83331e14b5]...circumstances in which such fees may be waived or reduced.

## 5.

Schedule 3 (documents and administration) has effect to specify—

- (a) the amount of the fees for specified applications and requests for the purposes of article 6 of the 2016 Order; and
- (b) exceptions to the requirement to pay fees referred to in paragraph (a), and [^c-88e79b086cbd884044beabdbdf9686f8]... circumstances in which such fees may be waived or reduced.

## 6.

Schedule 4 (sponsorship) has effect to specify the amount of the fees for specified applications, processes, services and premium services for sponsors in relation to sponsorship for the purposes of article 7 of the 2016 Order.

## 7.

Schedule 5 (consular functions) has effect to specify—

- (a) the amount of fees for specified consular functions for the purposes of article 8 of the 2016 Order; and
- (b) [^c-9244a524de248fd19b79ecc33443ed70]... circumstances in which such fees may be waived.

## 8.

Schedule 6 (premium services (in the United Kingdom)) has effect to specify—

- (a) the amount of the fees for specified premium services offered in the United Kingdom for the purposes of article 9 of the 2016 Order [^c22925151]; and
- (b) [^c-a7562e959e0059066e6c460140fd8fd8]... circumstances in which such fees may be waived or reduced.

## 9.

Schedule 7 (premium services (outside the United Kingdom)) has effect to specify—

- (a) the amount of the fees for specified premium services offered outside the United Kingdom for the purposes of article 9 of the 2016 Order; and
- (b) [^c-f607e0a18e8b6e17dd5e02e016cecf72]... circumstances in which such fees may be waived or reduced.

## 10.

Schedule 8 (nationality) has effect to specify—

- (a) the amount of the fees for—
  - (i) specified applications, processes and services in connection with nationality for the purposes of article 10 of the 2016 Order;
  - (ii)[^c-97dd854b09047e8b5ada1b1044152582] specified applications for certain documents and specified applications for the review of certain applications, for the purposes of article 6 of the 2016 Order;
- (aa)[^c-50c215c770fd09d06a627e20a4bbb625] exceptions to the requirements to pay specified fees referred to in paragraph (a);
- (b) the circumstances in which the fee for arrangement of a citizenship ceremony must be refunded; and[^c-1ccc5513b36620efc498127d1889d8ac]
- (c)[^c-65fa3e822dc72a6dceb14d9986c346e1] circumstances in which specified fees referred to in paragraph (a) may be waived.

## 11.

Schedule 9 (applications relating to entry clearance to enter the Isle of Man and premium services) has effect to specify—

- (a) the amount of the fees for specified applications for entry clearance to enter the Isle of Man [^c-348889c5ae8d33a9611be7acc3f1b98c]... for the purposes of articles 4, 5 [^c-721758660481d22beb75cfce242148f9]...[^c-93fd6e5e2a51abdfe8c5a2a9564e0a33] and 5C of the 2016 Order [^c22925161];
- (b) exceptions to the requirement to pay specified fees[^c-f5b8d9ddc0ee268080b611444ef56443] referred to in paragraph (a);
- (c) the amount of the fees for specified premium services offered outside the United Kingdom and the Isle of Man for the purposes of article 9A of the 2016 Order [^c22925171] as it relates to the Isle of Man; and
- (d) [^c-e6cf10196114720b84dffc06499c407e]... circumstances in which fees mentioned in paragraph (a) or (c) may be waived or reduced.

## 12.

Schedule 10 (applications for entry clearance to enter the Bailiwick of Guernsey or the Bailiwick of Jersey and premium services) has effect to specify—

- (a) the amount of the fees for specified applications for entry clearance to enter the Bailiwick of Guernsey or the Bailiwick of Jersey for the purposes of articles 4 , 5 and 5C[^c-ff916784b36ed7723d8bc41d7a63c9a1] of the 2016 Order [^c22925181];
- (b) the amount of the fees for specified premium services offered outside the United Kingdom and the Bailiwick of Guernsey or the Bailiwick of Jersey (as the case may be) for the purposes of article 9A of the 2016 Order as it relates to the Bailiwick of Guernsey and the Bailiwick of Jersey; and
- (c) [^c-fcbc49ed5ef6ceeab3745777256b3d66]... circumstances in which fees mentioned in paragraphs (a) and (b) may be waived or reduced.

## 13.

Schedule 11 (miscellaneous fees) has effect to specify—

- (a) the amount of the fees for—
  - (i) the administration of the Life in the UK test, for the purpose of fee 3.2.1 in the table in article 6 of the 2016 Order;
  - (ii) an administrative review of a decision for the purpose of article 6 of the 2016 Order; and
- (b) exceptions to the requirement to pay the fee referred to in (a)(ii), and [^c-9fcadbbe311fa4e4144142cb3e9eb28a]... circumstances in which such a fee may be waived or reduced.

## 13A. Windrush Scheme: power to waive fees[^c-2c95a869a1128c977cbd8abcd76da66b]

The Secretary of State may waive any fee specified in these Regulations which would otherwise be payable by a person for or in connection with an application made under the Windrush Scheme.

## 13B. Power to waive fees: exceptional circumstances affecting a number of individuals[^c-38cf89bd5e3bc3e0857e1904cbdf3619]

(1) Paragraph (2) applies where the Secretary of State considers that—

- (a) there are exceptional circumstances significantly affecting a number of individuals who are in the same or a similar situation, and
- (b) those circumstances are beyond the control of those individuals.

(2) Where the Secretary of State considers it appropriate to do so because of the effect of those circumstances on those individuals, the Secretary of State may decide, in relation to every one of those individuals, to waive the payment by them of any fee specified by these Regulations in respect of any particular description of application, request, process or service.

(3) The Secretary of State’s power under paragraph (2) is in addition to, and does not limit, the Secretary of State’s other powers under these Regulations to waive the payment of fees.

## 13C. Power to waive fees in consequence of a decision of a court or tribunal

(1) The Secretary of State may waive the payment of any fee specified in these Regulations where the Secretary of State considers it appropriate to do so in consequence of a decision of any court or tribunal in the United Kingdom.

(2) The Secretary of State’s power under paragraph (1) is in addition to, and does not limit, the Secretary of State’s other powers under these Regulations to waive the payment of fees.

## 13D. General power to refund fees

The Secretary of State may refund any fee, or any part of a fee, paid under these Regulations.

## 14. Amendment of the First-tier Tribunal (Immigration and Asylum Chamber) Fees Order 2011

Schedule 12 (amendment of the First-tier Tribunal (Immigration and Asylum Chamber) Fees Order 2011) [^c22925191] has effect.

## 15. Rate of exchange

Where a person seeks to pay any fee specified in these Regulations in a currency other than sterling (“the foreign currency”), the fee payable in the foreign currency is determined by reference to the Home Office Exchange Rate Policy [^c22925201] on the date that the payment is made.

## 16. Consequences of failing to pay the specified fee for an application

(1) Where a person is required to pay a fee specified in these Regulations for an application, but fails to pay that fee, the Secretary of State may—

- (a) reject the application as invalid; or
- (b) request the person to pay the outstanding amount.

(2) Where paragraph (1)(b) applies—

- (a) the person must pay the outstanding amount within 10 working days beginning with the day on which the request for payment was made;
- (b) if the outstanding amount is not paid within the period mentioned in sub-paragraph (a), the Secretary of State must reject the application as invalid.

(3) The period of 10 working days referred to in paragraph (2)(a) begins—

- (a) where the request for payment is made in writing, on the day that the request is sent;
- (b) where the request is made by telephone or in person, on the day that the request is made.

## 17. Payments for in person applications

(1) Where—

- (a) an application is made in person; and
- (b) the applicant pays the fee in relation to such an application prior to the date that the application is made,

the fee payable is that in relation to the relevant application on the date of payment.

(2) In this regulation, the date of payment means the date on which the payment is made by the applicant, unless it is made by post, in which case it means the date that the payment is posted.

## 18. Revocations

The following are revoked—

- (a) the Immigration and Nationality (Fees) Regulations 2017 [^c22925211] except regulation 14 of, and paragraph 1(1) and (3) of Schedule 12 to, those Regulations;
- (b) the Immigration and Nationality (Fees) (Amendment) Regulations 2017 [^c22925221].

Caroline Nokes

Minister of State

Home Office

## SCHEDULE 1 — APPLICATIONS FOR ENTRY CLEARANCE TO ENTER, AND LEAVE TO ENTER, THE UNITED KINGDOM[^c-2bd247e33b5d5c96a8c87326b2380ef9]

Regulation 3

### 1. Interpretation[^c-2bd247e33b5d5c96a8c87326b2380ef9]

(1)[^c-9b20370f296764ada6311c68e29a16cb] In this Schedule—

- “[^c-5b41511453e88328c4a0c48f07eb7c68]**HM Armed Forces service leaver**” means a person described in paragraph AF 3.1 together with paragraph AF 5.1 of Appendix HM Armed Forces of the immigration rules;
- “**liable to immigration detention**” means being liable to detention under—
  - (a) paragraph 16(1), (1A) or (2) of Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act [^c22925231];
  - (b) section 62 of the 2002 Act [^c22925241];
  - (c) paragraph 2(1), (2) or (3) of Schedule 3 to the 1971 Act [^c22925251]; or
  - (d) section 36(1) of the 2007 Act;
- “[^c-2a0d8be6741749711684237b056a59b9]**member of HM Armed Forces**” means a person described in paragraph AF 3.1 of Appendix HM Armed Forces of the immigration rules;
- [^c-87850fa6f5a5160a0a3929b990d150b4]...
- “**present and settled**” has the meaning given by paragraph 6 of the immigration rules;
- [^c-70897b73067a681a6aab22a5de52ec80]...
- [^c-70897b73067a681a6aab22a5de52ec80]...

(2)[^c-a5836d3d9098eca8ec2b386728f85bf9] In this Schedule, a reference to an application for entry clearance under an Appendix to the immigration rules being for entry clearance—

- (a) as the dependant partner or dependant child , or the dependent partner or dependent child,[^c-de4f8650e1d87bf4f6dceb58190395fb] of a particular description of person, or
- (b) as a dependant partner or dependant child or a dependent partner or dependent child[^c-f395cb76eda686460bed45302f9ab0fe], or any other description of dependant, on a particular route under that Appendix,

is to be construed in accordance with the Appendix in question.

(3)[^c-7cd18a340f4231792b975f05d414b47c] For the purposes of sub-paragraph (2) there is to be regarded as being no difference between—

- (a) the expressions “dependant partner” and “dependent partner”, where the reference in this Schedule uses one of those expressions and the Appendix in question uses the other, or
- (b) the expressions “dependant child” and “dependent child”, where the reference in this Schedule uses one of those expressions and the Appendix in question uses the other.

### 2. Fees for, and in connection with, applications for entry clearance to enter and leave to enter the United Kingdom[^c-2bd247e33b5d5c96a8c87326b2380ef9]

(1) Table 1 specifies the amount of—

- (a) the fees for the specified applications for entry clearance to enter or leave to enter the United Kingdom; and
- (b) the fees for the specified applications[^c-00abcdb59cf2971e896c2115252fed2d] for an approval letter from an endorsing body[^c-dd10500a272c2c83844f48d21d72ef39].

(2) Table 2 specifies the amount of the fees for specified applications for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom as the dependant of a main applicant.

(3) Table 3 specifies the amount of the fee for an application for entry clearance and[^c-88d8415546895989c6848849d78ff856] indefinite leave to enter the United Kingdom as the dependant of an HM Armed Forces service leaver or a member of HM Armed Forces[^c-3482fcc6299849a40cb4c8be4643b8a4].

(4) Table 4 provides for exceptions to the requirement to pay fees specified in Tables 1, 2 and 3, and Table 5 provides for the waiver or reduction of fees specified in Tables 1, 2 and 3 in specified circumstances (and see paragraph 3A)[^c-83a5f29205d81f1153966419cabff076].

(5) Paragraph 3 makes provision for the amount of fees to be paid in respect of an application for entry clearance to enter or leave to enter the United Kingdom by a dependant of a main applicant in cases where the fees specified in 1.3C.1, 1.3C.2,[^c-8c01dad9ff6a884ad37e7b6750d1f527] 1.4.1 or 1.4.5 of Table 1 or in Table 2 or Table 3 do not apply.

(5A)[^c-416c7f45919eeb0db8a836bd519df0f6] Paragraph 3A provides for the reduction in specified circumstances of fees for applications for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom under Appendix Skilled Worker to the immigration rules[^c-8110ac29ce98aff35c9df19511a09574].

(6)[^c-50e7113a42db0c6c7981e88c8910e931] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(7) Each of the fees specified in 1.1.2 to 1.1.4 and 1.1.8 in Table 1 is calculated by reference to an annual rate.

(8) The annual rate referred to in sub-paragraph (7) for each of the fees in question is determined by dividing the amount of the fee by the number of years to which the application relates.

[^c-df83cfc9988e2c1e8076f2efc4300631][^c-f1e799873fa5cb1d6c4a29c7ba68218a][^c-66fdbcba9094af09e7cda652451ef3ca][^c-98f24e9ba74b6b64d760fabd5557cf21]

Table 4 (Exceptions in respect of fees for and in connection with[^c-d4bb7cd5e3709f63b932fa104ad220cb] applications for entry clearance to enter or leave to enter the United Kingdom)

<table>
<tbody>
<tr><th colspan="2"><em>Number and description of the exception</em></th><th><em>Fees to which exception applies</em></th></tr>
<tr><th><strong>4.1</strong></th><th colspan="2"><strong>Officials of Her Majesty's Government</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td>4.1.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application made in connection with the official duty of any official of Her Majesty's Government.</td><td>All fees in Tables 1, 2 and 3</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>4.2</strong></th><th colspan="2"><strong>Dependants of refugees or persons granted humanitarian protection</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td>4.2.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application made under Appendix Family Reunion (Sponsors with Protection) of the immigration rules.</td><td>Fee 1.5.1</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>4.3</strong></th><th colspan="2"><strong>Applications under Appendix ECAA Extension of Stay to the immigration rules</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td>4.3.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application made under Appendix ECAA Extension of Stay to the immigration rules.</td><td>Fee 1.5.1</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>4.4</strong></th><th colspan="2"><strong>Applications for limited leave to enter the United Kingdom (by applicants physically present in the United Kingdom but liable to immigration detention) where to require payment of the fee would be incompatible with the applicant's Convention rights</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td>4.4.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application for limited leave to enter the United Kingdom, made by an applicant physically present in the United Kingdom but liable to immigration detention, where to require payment of the fee would be incompatible with the applicant's Convention rights.</td><td>Fee 1.4.7</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>4.4A</strong></td><td><strong>Applications for limited leave to enter the United Kingdom made solely or primarily on the basis of the applicant’s Convention rights by applicants who are persons in immigration detention or who are in the Family Returns Process</strong></td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>4.4A.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application for limited leave to enter the United Kingdom made solely or primarily on the basis of the applicant’s Convention rights by an applicant who is—<br>(a) a person in immigration detention, or<br>(b) in the Family Returns Process.</td><td>Fee 1.4.7</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>4.5</strong></td><td colspan="2"><strong>Applications for entry clearance under Appendix EU (Family Permit) to the immigration rules</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td>4.5.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom made under Appendix EU (Family Permit) to the immigration rules.</td><td>Fees 1.4.4 and 1.5.1</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>4.6</strong></td><td colspan="2"><strong>Applications for entry clearance ... under Appendix Afghan Relocation and Assistance Policy (ARAP) to the immigration rules</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td>4.6.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application under Appendix Afghan Relocation and Assistance Policy (ARAP) to the immigration rules for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom ...</td><td>Fees 1.4.4 and 1.5.1</td></tr>
<tr><td>. . .</td><td colspan="2">. . .</td></tr>
<tr><td>. . .</td><td>. . .</td><td>. . .</td></tr>
<tr><td>4.8</td><td colspan="2">Applications for entry clearance under Appendix S2 Healthcare Visitor to the immigration rules</td></tr>
<tr><td>4.8.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom made under Appendix S2 Healthcare Visitor to the immigration rules.</td><td>Fees 1.1.1, 1.1.6 and 1.5.1</td></tr>
<tr><td>. . .</td><td>. . .</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>. . .</td><td>. . .</td><td>. . .</td></tr>
<tr><td>. . .</td><td>. . .</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>. . .</td><td>. . .</td><td>. . .</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>4.11</strong></td><td colspan="2"><strong>Applications for entry clearance and indefinite leave to enter by certain HM Armed Forces service leavers and members of HM Armed Forces</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td>4.11.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application for entry clearance and indefinite leave to enter the United Kingdom made under Appendix HM Armed Forces to the immigration rules if—<br>(a) in a case where the application is made on the basis of the completion of a period of reckonable service in HM Forces, the applicant’s period of reckonable service is at least 6 years, or<br>(b) in a case where the application is made on the basis of meeting the medical discharge criteria, the cause was attributable to service in HM Forces<br>(with expressions used in (a) and (b) being construed in accordance with that Appendix).</td><td>1.3D</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>4.12</strong></td><td colspan="2"><strong>Applications for entry clearance under Appendix Ukraine Scheme to the immigration rules</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td>4.12.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom under Appendix Ukraine Scheme to the immigration rules.</td><td>Fee 1.5.1</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>4.13</strong></td><td colspan="2"><strong>Applications for an approval letter from the Global Entrepreneur Programme in respect of a proposed application under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td>4.13.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application for an approval letter from an endorsing body, in respect of a proposed application for entry clearance under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules where the endorsing body from which the letter is sought is the Global Entrepreneur Programme operated by the Department for Business and Trade.</td><td>1.3A.A1</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>4.14</strong></td><td colspan="2"><strong>Applications, in specified circumstances, for an approval letter from an endorsing body or a legacy endorsing body in respect of a proposed application under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td>4.14.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application for an approval letter from an endorsing body or from a legacy endorsing body in respect of a proposed application for entry clearance under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules where—<br>(a) the applicant has limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom which was granted under Appendix Innovator, Appendix Innovator Founder or Appendix Start-up to the immigration rules (“the applicant’s current leave”),<br>(b) the endorsing body or legacy endorsing body from which the approval letter is sought by the applicant endorsed the applicant (pursuant to the immigration rules) on or before 12th April 2023 in connection with the application which gave rise to the grant of the applicant’s ... leave, and<br>(c) where the applicant has been granted leave, or further leave, under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules (“the relevant AIF leave”), the applicant has had previous leave granted, before the grant of the relevant AIF leave, under—<br>(i) Appendix Innovator or Appendix Start-up to the immigration rules; or<br>(ii) Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules, in a case where the leave was granted following an endorsement given on or before 12th April 2023</td><td>1.3A.A1</td></tr>
<tr><td>4.14.2</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application for an approval letter from an endorsing body, in respect of a proposed application for entry clearance under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules where—<br>(a) the applicant has limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom which was granted under Appendix Innovator, Appendix Innovator Founder or Appendix Start-up to the immigration rules (“the applicant’s current leave”),<br>(b) the endorsing body from which the approval letter is sought by the applicant did not endorse the applicant (pursuant to the immigration rules) in connection with the application which gave rise to the grant of the applicant’s current leave,<br>(c) the letter which endorsed the applicant in connection with the applicant’s current leave (“the original letter”) was issued (pursuant to the immigration rules) by a legacy endorsing body which was at the time when the original letter was issued, approved by the Home Office to endorse individuals in connection with applications under Appendix Innovator to those rules, and<br>(d) the applicant cannot obtain an approval letter from that legacy endorsing body as that body no longer endorses individuals or businesses in connection with applications under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules.</td><td>1.3A.A1</td></tr>
<tr><td>4.15</td><td>Applications for entry clearance as a VIP Delegate under Appendix VIP Delegate Visa to the immigration rules</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>4.15.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom as a VIP Delegate under Appendix VIP Delegate Visa to the immigration rules.</td><td>Fee 1.5.1</td></tr>
<tr><td>4.16</td><td>Applications for entry clearance as a Diplomatic Visa Arrangement Visitor under Appendix V:Visitor to the immigration rules</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>4.16.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom as a Diplomatic Visa Arrangement Visitor under Appendix V:Visitor to the immigration rules.</td><td>Fee 1.1.2</td></tr>
<tr><td>4.17</td><td>Applications under Appendix UK/European Applicant Transfer Scheme</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>4.17.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom under Appendix UK/European Applicant Transfer Scheme to the immigration rules.</td><td>Fee 1.5.1</td></tr>
<tr><td>4.18</td><td>Applications under Appendix Statelessness</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>4.18.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application under Appendix Statelessness to the immigration rules for entry clearance to enter or leave to enter the United Kingdom as—<br>(a) a partner of a stateless person, or<br>(b) a child of a stateless person.</td><td>Fee 1.4.4</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

[^c-5dea68e41f732b56db71bf0228646bda][^c-14a2a81469921a9760ff6056f683b62b][^c-80af1f24689eb35f4cda94c14063d6f9][^c-05d2e51099edae6a06fc878aee33d708][^c-e6cf4bc27eef23ac2df12e6a0b18a880][^c-c9b38f99bcb8431f87099de8792b0fe2][^c-762f48933452be83737c8286e2c232f6][^c-490751b6caab962708c5967dac897208][^c-df2dd94a52edc7daab88296cfd3ec225][^c-d0ad8efb47414240f05cd762340ccfd8][^c-897eeab7486f793088bb0b811a71556b][^c-d0ad8efb47414240f05cd762340ccfd8][^c-d0ad8efb47414240f05cd762340ccfd8][^c-d0ad8efb47414240f05cd762340ccfd8][^c-d0ad8efb47414240f05cd762340ccfd8][^c-2298489ceae138e45759a3ba699a00f0][^c-2298489ceae138e45759a3ba699a00f0][^c-2298489ceae138e45759a3ba699a00f0][^c-2298489ceae138e45759a3ba699a00f0][^c-2298489ceae138e45759a3ba699a00f0][^c-6902e834b955fc0375cb4e9a74975aae][^c-6902e834b955fc0375cb4e9a74975aae][^c-6902e834b955fc0375cb4e9a74975aae][^c-6902e834b955fc0375cb4e9a74975aae][^c-6902e834b955fc0375cb4e9a74975aae][^c-113130489c94128969371495db1d5728][^c-1f56c33970e2e735db8e9323512f36c2][^c-52f7ad86faf111cc43eaa9081ef4dab0][^c-d950c9e7e553a0776d810d586e7ba14e][^c-16f47e3205cc23ba4070e5eb57cecc6d][^c-a2a38a7e8e15559ed06b61902e5505fe][^c-5bfb42da783c3418d02357dc211805d6][^c-6b34136760ba2a5eaa53e9e6bf5f4829][^c-d72f2286e9229fe5b20e472e0d37de05][^c-07812232ca98ea991c4c9ccd6d7d9dae][^c-581bbcd5592ce28da1eb73b749013151]

Table 5 (Waivers or reductions in respect of fees for applications for entry clearance to enter or leave to enter the United Kingdom)

<table>
<tbody>
<tr><th colspan="2"><em>Number and description of the waiver or reduction</em></th><th><em>Fees to which waiver or reduction applies</em></th></tr>
<tr><th><strong>5.1</strong></th><th colspan="2"><strong>General waiver</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td>5.1.1</td><td>No fee is payable in respect of an application where the Secretary of State determines that the fee should be waived.</td><td>All fees in Tables 1, 2 and 3</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>5.2</strong></th><th colspan="2"><strong>Scholarships funded by Her Majesty's Government</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td>5.2.1</td><td>The official determining an application may decide to waive the payment of the fee or reduce the amount of the fee where the application is made by a candidate for, or holder of, a scholarship funded by Her Majesty's Government and is in connection with such a scholarship.</td><td>Fees 1.1.1, 1.1.8, 1.2.2 and 1.3.11A</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>5.3</strong></th><th colspan="2"><strong>International courtesy</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td>5.3.1</td><td>The official determining an application may decide to waive the payment of the fee or reduce the amount of the fee as a matter of international courtesy.</td><td>All fees in Tables 1, 2 and 3</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>5.4</strong></th><th colspan="2"><strong>Visitors under a Foreign, Commonwealth and Development Office Bilateral Programme</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td>5.4.1</td><td>The official determining an application may decide to waive the payment of the fee or reduce the amount of the fee where the applicant intends to visit the United Kingdom, in connection with programmes operated by the Foreign, Commonwealth and Development Office to give funds directly to Embassies and Missions outside the United Kingdom, to support activities directly connected to the United Kingdom's international priorities.</td><td>All fees in Tables 1, 2 and 3</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>5.5</strong></th><th colspan="2"><strong>Visitors under a Foreign, Commonwealth and Development Office Strategic Programme</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td>5.5.1</td><td>The official determining an application may decide to waive the payment of the fee or reduce the amount of the fee where the applicant intends to visit the United Kingdom, in connection with programmes of funding operated by the Foreign, Commonwealth and Development Office to promote action on global issues in areas of strategic importance to the United Kingdom.</td><td>All fees in Tables 1, 2 and 3</td></tr>
<tr><td>5.6</td><td>Victims of domestic abuse</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>5.6.1</td><td>The official determining an application for entry clearance and indefinite leave to enter under the Appendix Victim of Domestic Abuse to the immigration rules may decide to waive the fee if at the time of making the application the applicant appears to the official to be destitute.</td><td>Fee 1.3E.1</td></tr>
<tr><td>5.7</td><td>Bereaved partners</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>5.7.1</td><td>The official determining an application for entry clearance and indefinite leave to enter under Appendix Bereaved Partner or Appendix Gurkha and Hong Kong military unit veteran discharged before 1 July 1997 to the immigration rules may decide to waive the fee if at the time of making the application the applicant appears to the official to be destitute.</td><td>Fee 1.4.4A and 3.1.1</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

[^c-65057d031802c2aac075e9e7f3b55b21][^c-9f15e1b981a59da14c4564e9d274a977][^c-102f738a0963070d5dc66128ddaef62a][^c-437f106c3ba1737d40d706137fb1fb26][^c-50c1a1d23cc15b57009526283d381697]

### 3. Applications by dependants[^c-f453bce571a0e68e394da3565428575e]

(1) The fee for an application for entry clearance to enter or leave to enter the United Kingdom made by the dependant of a main applicant is—

- (a) where the dependant’s application is not made at the same time as the main applicant’s application, the fee paid by the main applicant under Table 1 in respect of their last application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom which was made before the dependant’s application;
- (b) in any other case, the fee specified in Table 1 in respect of the main applicant’s application.

(2) Sub-paragraph (1) does not apply in respect of applications for which a fee is specified in—

- (a) 1.3C.1, 1.3C.2, 1.4.1 or 1.4.5 of Table 1;
- (b) Table 2; or
- (c) Table 3.

(3) The fee payable in accordance with sub-paragraph (1) is subject to—

- (a) the exceptions and waivers set out in Tables 4 and 5; and
- (b) paragraph 3A.

### 3A. Reduction of fees for Health and Care Visa applications and related applications by dependants[^c-d959e871608f43933195e48b0ffa0a1b][^c-2bd247e33b5d5c96a8c87326b2380ef9]

(1) In this paragraph—

- a “Health and Care Visa application” means an application for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom under Appendix Skilled Worker to the immigration rules, other than as the dependant partner or dependant child of a Skilled Worker, where the certificate of sponsorship issued , or the approval under Sponsor a Worker given,[^c-5ae82993ce234876473fe37206e494f4] in respect of the application confirms the applicant’s eligibility for a Health and Care Visa provided for by the Health and Care Visa Guidance;
- a “relevant dependant’s application” means an application for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom under Appendix Skilled Worker to the immigration rules as the dependant partner or dependant child of a Skilled Worker where—
  - (a) the certificate of sponsorship issued , or the approval under Sponsor a Worker given, in respect of the application by the main applicant confirms the main applicant’s eligibility for a Health and Care Visa provided for by the Health and Care Visa Guidance, or by the Tier 2 Policy Guidance where the main applicant’s application was made before 9.00 a.m. on 1st December 2020, or
  - (b) the applicant has a sponsor’s letter or email referred to in paragraph 9 of the Health and Care Visa Guidance.

(2) In the case of a Health and Care Visa application, the fee under 1.3.6A, 1.3.6B, 1.3.6C or 1.3.6D of Table 1 payable in respect of an application for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom under Appendix Skilled Worker to the immigration rules is to be reduced—

- (a) by £495[^c-13af0f40d772d9ce26a813c92b077efb], where it is the fee specified by 1.3.6A of that Table;
- (b) by £990[^c-7ec9ff7f1949b824162bee58d15e6c5f], where it is the fee specified by 1.3.6B of that Table;
- (c)[^c-bdade25dcc9851130d2cbdff737b2244] by £304[^c-34718f85e837b99d92bfe2a10c2f13d5], where it is the fee specified by 1.3.6C of that Table;
- (d) by £607[^c-5ccbccdd0c3bdd186d20f2093b0da39a], where it is the fee specified by 1.3.6D of that Table.

(3) In the case of a relevant dependant’s application where the application by the main applicant was made at or after 9.00 a.m. on 1st December 2020, the fee payable by virtue of paragraph 3 in respect of an application for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom under Appendix Skilled Worker to the immigration rules as the dependant partner or dependant child of a Skilled Worker is to be reduced—

- (a) by £495, where it is the fee specified by 1.3.6A of that Table;
- (b) by £990, where it is the fee specified by 1.3.6B of that Table;
- (c)[^c-82b0dae1df53bcc8ead94afec4caf748] by £304, where it is the fee specified by 1.3.6C of that Table;
- (d) by £607, where it is the fee specified by 1.3.6D of that Table.

(4) In the case of a relevant dependant’s application where the application by the main applicant was made before 9.00 a.m. on 1st December 2020, the fee payable by virtue of paragraph 3 in respect of an application for entry clearance to enter the United Kingdom under Appendix Skilled Worker to the immigration rules as the dependant partner or dependant child of a Skilled Worker is to be reduced—

- (a) by £495, where it is the fee specified by 1.3.7 of Table 1 as it continues to have effect by virtue of regulation 14(1) of the Immigration and Nationality (Replacement of Tier 2 and Fees) (Amendment) (EU Exit) Regulations 2020;
- (b) by £990, where it is the fee specified by 1.3.8 of that Table as it continues to have effect by virtue of that regulation;
- (c)[^c-385d6b2742d9f5fce1110440a3cac756] by £304, where it is the fee specified by 1.3.9 of that Table as it continues to have effect by virtue of that regulation (and as modified by paragraph 2(4) of Schedule 2 to the Immigration and Nationality and Immigration Services Commissioner (Fees) (Amendment) Regulations 2022);
- (d) by £607, where it is the fee specified by 1.3.10 of that Table as it continues to have effect by virtue of that regulation (and as modified by paragraph 2(5) of Schedule 2 to Immigration and Nationality and Immigration Services Commissioner (Fees) (Amendment) Regulations 2022.

(5) In sub-paragraph (1)—

- (a) the references to “the Health and Care Visa Guidance” are references to the document entitled “Health and Care Visa Guidance” published by the Home Office;
- (b) the reference to “the Tier 2 Policy Guidance” is a reference to the document entitled “Tier 2 of the Points-Based System – Policy Guidance” published by the Home Office.

### 4. Applications by CESC Nationals[^c-1031d6f5300c5338152e4bc9dc131715][^c-2bd247e33b5d5c96a8c87326b2380ef9]

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

## SCHEDULE 2 — APPLICATIONS FOR LEAVE TO REMAIN IN THE UNITED KINGDOM[^c-36d503148380d5988764d64a7d49acfc]

Regulation 4

### 1. Interpretation[^c-36d503148380d5988764d64a7d49acfc]

(1) In this Schedule—

- “**Article 3 or Refugee Convention application**” means an application for leave to remain in the United Kingdom made on the basis that the applicant is—
  - (a) a person making a claim for asylum under Part 11 of the immigration rules which has either not been determined or has been granted;
  - (b) a person who has been granted humanitarian protection under that Part of the immigration rules;
  - (c) a person claiming that, due to an ongoing medical condition, removal from the United Kingdom would be incompatible with Article 3 of the European Convention on Human Rights;
  - (d) a person who is [^c-0292aadb5310a7f02cad37888b450328]... applying for leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Family Reunion (Sponsors with Protection)[^c-4427b9e0bd3b6caabb37a7c5000335f7][^c-135dfae2b7601a9a4002f1f0e5e65b8a] to the immigration rules; or
  - (e) a child who was born in the United Kingdom to a person referred to in paragraph (a) or (b);
- “**claim for asylum**” has the meaning given by section 94(1) of the 1999 Act [^c22925271];
- [^c-da06752cab8f1afa3fea4228c9f4d8a6]...
- “**specified human rights application**” means an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under—
  - (a) Appendix Private Life to the immigration rules;[^c-0a74ebbd5f4b19cd013a9c2dba95a79b]
  - (b) section R-LTRP.1.1. (a), (b) , (c)[^c-22c0e336194175e802464a8c322d6342] and (d) of Appendix FM to the immigration rules;
  - (c) section R-LTRPT.1.1. (a), (b) , (c)[^c-1094b34a0f450ada873a2ac4ad44bf37] and (d) of Appendix FM to the immigration rules; [^c-746ad7c1f1614d62ab343ab28ff39d8c]...
  - (ca) Appendix Child Relative (Sponsors with Protection)[^c-5904aa2d0614b0ec5627dc53cbbe57f2][^c-fefff4d916f25728846eff27b2bae25c] to the immigration rules; or
  - (d) any other application other than an Article 3 or Refugee Convention application in which the applicant relies solely or primarily on a claim that to remove the applicant from the United Kingdom or to require the applicant to leave the United Kingdom would be unlawful under section 6 of the Human Rights Act 1998 [^c22925291] (public authority not to act contrary to Convention rights);
- [^c-da06752cab8f1afa3fea4228c9f4d8a6]...
- [^c-da06752cab8f1afa3fea4228c9f4d8a6]...

(2) For the purposes of this Schedule a claim for asylum is determined on—

- (a) the day on which the Secretary of State notifies the claimant of the decision on the claim;
- (b) if the claimant has appealed against the Secretary of State's decision, the day on which the appeal is disposed of; or
- (c) if the claimant has brought an appeal from within the United Kingdom under section 82 of the 2002 Act [^c22925321] or section 2 of the Special Immigration Appeals Commission Act 1997 [^c22925331], the day on which the appeal is disposed of.

(2A)[^c-e09566dd3bd3674598456786fc3cd773] In this Schedule, a reference to an application for limited leave to remain under an Appendix to the immigration rules being for limited leave to remain—

- (a) as the dependant partner or dependant child , or the dependent partner or dependent child,[^c-188bcbe6e521d8b0fbcaf25d870832c1] of a particular description of person, or
- (b) as a dependant partner or dependant child or a dependent partner or dependent child[^c-f0ceee2ef38ecb750f16304ee56a4f3f], or any other description of dependant, on a particular route under that Appendix,

is to be construed in accordance with the Appendix in question.

(3)[^c-07661b6f892ef67f571de8323a074795] For the purposes of sub-paragraph (2A) there is to be regarded as being no difference between—

- (a) the expressions “dependant partner” and “dependent partner”, where the reference in this Schedule uses one of those expressions and the Appendix in question uses the other, or
- (b) the expressions “dependant child” and “dependent child”, where the reference in this Schedule uses one of those expressions and the Appendix in question uses the other.

### 2. Fees for, and in connection with, applications for leave to remain in the United Kingdom[^c-36d503148380d5988764d64a7d49acfc]

(1) Table 6 specifies the amount of the fees for the specified applications for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom and the amount of the fees for the specified applications for an approval letter from an endorsing body in respect of an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom[^c-a0b49232aac92b48202f5de0b7fe8906].

(2) Table 7 specifies the amount of the fees for the specified applications for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom as the dependant of a main applicant.

(3) Table 8 specifies the amount of the fee for an application for indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom [^c-b28ac6e5f0942832ea8b6ee7053585f0]... and the amount of the fee for an application for an approval letter from an endorsing body in respect of a specified application for indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom[^c-70aeeb20ec8cc1562c529ced87561f5d].

(3A)[^c-4715b586e89f92bf85cc35d51436b8b9] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(4) Table 9 provides for exceptions to and waivers of the requirement to pay certain fees specified in Tables 6, 7 and 8[^c-42e5b0974d64f70dbe648a55fca574a4].

(5) Paragraph 3 makes provision for the amount of the fees to be paid in respect of an application for leave to remain in the United Kingdom by a dependant of a main applicant, in cases where the fees specified in 6.2C.1 or 6.2C.2 of Table 6 or in Table 7 do not apply[^c-96bf2c9818c7cfdcaf64e1802fd93427].

(5A)[^c-0bb9d5e3d4dceef6057a026016335f84] Paragraph 3A provides for the reduction in specified circumstances of fees for applications for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Skilled Worker to the immigration rules[^c-0445773b98c42260b8fff3f0761e4499].

(6) [^c-19aa2c5f718fc99aeba769cece62be8b]... The fees specified in Tables 6, 7 and 8[^c-5a58a756f9d6645ee9b90544405a0073] are subject to paragraph 5 (variation of an application for leave to remain in the United Kingdom).

<tr><td><strong>9.4A</strong></td><td colspan="2"><strong>Applications for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom made solely or primarily on the basis of the applicant’s Convention rights by applicants who are persons in immigration detention or who are in the Family Returns Process</strong></td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom made solely or primarily on the basis of the applicant’s Convention rights by an applicant who is—<br>(a) a person in immigration detention, or<br>(b) in the Family Returns Process.</td><td>Fee 6.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>9.5</strong></th><th colspan="3"><strong>Short term variation of leave to remain in the United Kingdom</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application made to an immigration officer on arrival at a port of entry in the United Kingdom in respect of a person seeking variation of leave to remain in the United Kingdom for a period of up to six months.</td><td>Fees 6.2.11A, 6.2.6F, 6.3.1 ...</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>9.6</strong></th><th colspan="3"><strong>Children being looked after by a local authority</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application made in respect of a person who, at the time of making the application, is a child and is being looked after by a local authority.</td><td>Fees 6.1.1, 6.2.11A, 6.2.6F, 6.2.6H, 6.2.6I, 8.1.1 . . .</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>9.7</strong></th><th colspan="3"><strong>Applications under Appendix ECAA Extension of Stay to the immigration rules</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application made under Appendix ECAA Extension of Stay to the immigration rules.</td><td>Fee 6.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>9.8</strong></th><th colspan="3"><strong>Applications from stateless persons</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for limited leave to remain or indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom (together referred to in this entry as “leave to remain”)—<br>(a) under Appendix Statelessness to the immigration rules, as a stateless person;<br>(b) under Appendix Statelessness to the immigration rules, as a partner or child of a stateless person;</td><td>Fees 6.1.1, 8.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>9.9</strong></th><th colspan="3"><strong>Applications for variation of limited leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom to allow recourse to public funds</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for variation of the conditions attached to a grant of limited leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom, in order to be permitted access to public funds, by a person who has been granted such leave (subject to a condition of no recourse or access to public funds).</td><td>Fees 6.1.1, 6.2C.1, 6.2C.2</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.9A</strong></td><td colspan="2"><strong>Certain applications for leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Hong Kong British National (Overseas) of the immigration rules</strong></td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for leave to remain in the United Kingdom for 30 months under Appendix Hong Kong British National (Overseas) to the immigration rules where, at the time the application is made—<br>(a) the applicant has leave to enter or leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Hong Kong British National (Overseas) of the immigration rules,<br>(b) any condition of no access to public funds to which that leave was subject has been removed in accordance with paragraph HK 65.1 of that Appendix,<br>(c) the applicant is in receipt of public funds, and<br>(d) the applicant is, in the opinion of the Secretary of State, unable to afford the fee.</td><td>6.2C.1</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>9.10</strong></th><th colspan="3"><strong>Grant of leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Temporary Permission to Stay for Victims of Human Trafficking or Slavery to the immigration rules</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td>9.10.1</td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of the first grant of leave under Appendix Temporary Permission to Stay for Victims of Human Trafficking or Slavery to the immigration rules) (“the Appendix”) (whether or not an application is made for such leave to be granted).</td><td>Fee 6.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><td>9.10.2</td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of a further grant of leave under the Appendix where the individual—<br>(a) has not yet accrued in total thirty months limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom, granted as discretionary leave or under the Appendix; or<br>(b) was not originally granted thirty months limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom (granted as discretionary leave or under the Appendix) and the application is to allow the individual to have in total thirty months limited leave.<br>.</td><td>Fee 6.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><td>9.10.3</td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application made under the Appendix where—<br>(a) 9.10.2 does not apply because the individual has accrued—<br>(i) thirty months or more limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom granted as discretionary leave;<br>(ii) thirty months or more limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom granted under the Appendix; or<br>(iii) a total of thirty months or more limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom granted as discretionary leave or under the Appendix; and<br>(b) at the time of making the application the individual appears to the Secretary of State to be destitute.</td><td>Fee 6.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><td>9.10.4</td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable by a dependant of a person granted an exception under 9.10.1 to 9.10.3.</td><td>Fee 6.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>9.11</strong></th><th colspan="3"><strong>Applications for leave as a domestic worker who is the victim of slavery or human trafficking</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td>9.11.1</td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for leave to remain made under Appendix Domestic Worker who is a Victim of Modern Slavery to the immigration rules.</td><td>Fee 6.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><th><strong>9.12</strong></th><th colspan="3"><strong>Applications by qualifying residents of Grenfell Tower and Grenfell Walk</strong></th></tr>
<tr><td>9.12.1</td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for leave to remain made under the Grenfell Tower Immigration Cases Policy for qualifying residents of Grenfell Tower and Grenfell Walk .</td><td>Fees 6.1.1, 8.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><td>9.12.2</td><td colspan="2">The official determining an application for leave to remain may decide to waive the payment of the fee where the application is made (on or after the day on which these Regulations come into force)—<br>(a) by a person who, on 31st January 2018, was eligible to be considered under the policy referred to in 9.12.1; or<br>(b) in respect of a child born on or after 14th June 2017 in the United Kingdom to a person who was so eligible on 31st January 2018 (whether or not the person had come forward for consideration under the policy by that date).</td><td>Fees 6.1.1, 8.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>. . .</strong></td><td colspan="3"><strong>. . .</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.14</strong></td><td colspan="2"><strong>Applications for leave to remain in the United Kingdom by relevant Afghan citizens and their dependants</strong></td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>9.14.1</td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom made under paragraph 305 of the immigration rules in respect of a child of a person given limited leave to enter the United Kingdom as a relevant Afghan citizen under paragraph 276BA1 of the immigration rules before 6th October 2021.</td><td>Fee 6.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><td>9.14.2</td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application under Appendix Afghan Relocation and Assistance Policy (ARAP) to the immigration rules for indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom ...</td><td>Fee 8.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.15</strong></td><td colspan="2"><strong>Applications for indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom by persons relocated to the United Kingdom under section 67 of the Immigration Act 2016</strong> <strong>(unaccompanied refugee children: relocation and support)</strong></td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom made under paragraph 352ZN of the immigration rules.</td><td>Fee 8.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.16</strong></td><td colspan="3"><strong>Applications for leave to remain in the United Kingdom made under Appendix EU to the immigration rules</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for leave to remain in the United Kingdom made under Appendix EU to the immigration rules.</td><td>Fees 6.1.1, 8.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.17</strong></td><td colspan="3"><strong>Applications for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom made under Appendix S2 Healthcare Visitor to the immigration rules</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom made under Appendix S2 Healthcare Visitor to the immigration rules.</td><td>Fees 6.1.1, 6.3.3</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.18</strong></td><td colspan="3"><strong>Applications for indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom by certain HM Armed Forces service leavers and members of HM Armed Forces</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom made under Appendix HM Armed Forces to the immigration rules if—<br>(a) in a case where the application is made on the basis of the completion of a period of reckonable service in HM Forces, the applicant’s period of reckonable service is at least 6 years, or<br>(b) in a case where the application is made on the basis of meeting the medical discharge criteria, the cause was attributable to service in HM Forces<br>(with expressions used in (a) and (b) being construed in accordance with that Appendix).</td><td>Fee 8.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.19</strong></td><td colspan="3"><strong>Applications for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules resulting from variation by the Secretary of State of an application for indefinite leave to remain</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules arising by virtue of the variation by the Secretary of State, under paragraph INNF 21.2 or INNF 41.2 of that Appendix, of an application for indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom.</td><td>Fee 6.2A.1</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.20</strong></td><td colspan="3"><strong>Applications for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Hong Kong British National (Overseas) to the immigration rules resulting from variation by the Secretary of State of an application for indefinite leave to remain</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Hong Kong British National (Overseas) to the immigration rules arising by virtue of the variation by the Secretary of State, under paragraph HK 64.1A of that Appendix, of an application for indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom.</td><td>Fees 6.2C.1, 6.2C.2</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.21</strong></td><td colspan="3"><strong>Applications for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Private Life to the immigration rules resulting from variation by the Secretary of State of an application for indefinite leave to remain</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Private Life to the immigration rules arising by virtue of the variation by the Secretary of State, under paragraph PL 18.2 or PL 33.2 of that Appendix, of an application for indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom.</td><td>Fee 6.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.22</strong></td><td colspan="3"><strong>Applications for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix FM to the immigration rules resulting from variation by the Secretary of State of an application for indefinite leave to remain under Appendix Settlement Family Life to those rules</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix FM to the immigration rules arising by virtue of the variation by the Secretary of State, under paragraph SETF 9.2 or SETF 17.2 of Appendix Settlement Family Life to those rules, of an application for indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Settlement Family Life to those rules.</td><td>Fee 6.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.22A</strong></td><td colspan="2"><strong>Applications for leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Long Residence to the immigration rules resulting from a variation by the Secretary of State of an application for settlement</strong></td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Long Residence to the immigration rules arising from a variation by the Secretary of State, under paragraph LR 15.2 of that Appendix, of an application for settlement in the United Kingdom</td><td>Fee 6.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.23</strong></td><td colspan="3"><strong>Applications for indefinite or limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom: waiver of fee as a remedy for maladministration</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">The Secretary of State may waive the payment of any fee specified in Table 6, 7 or 8 in respect of an application for limited or indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom where the Secretary of State considers that—<br>(a) the person in respect of whom the application is made has suffered any detriment in connection with immigration or nationality as a result of maladministration by the Home Office, and<br>(b) the appropriate remedy (or part of the appropriate remedy) for that maladministration is to waive the payment of the fee in respect of the application.</td><td>All Fees in Tables 6, 7 and 8</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.24</strong></td><td colspan="3"><strong>Applications for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Ukraine Scheme to the immigration rules</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Ukraine Scheme to the immigration rules.</td><td>Fee 6.1.1</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.25</strong></td><td colspan="3"><strong>Applications for an approval letter from the Global Entrepreneur Programme in respect of a proposed application for leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for an approval letter from an endorsing body in respect of an application for limited or indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules where the endorsing body from which the letter is sought is the Global Entrepreneur Programme operated by the Department for Business and Trade.</td><td>Fees 6.2A.A1, 8.2.1</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9.26</strong></td><td colspan="3"><strong>Applications, in specified circumstances, for an approval letter from an endorsing body or a legacy endorsing body in respect of a proposed application for leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td>9.26.1</td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for an approval letter from an endorsing body or legacy endorsing body in respect of a proposed application for limited or indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules where—<br>(a) the applicant has limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom which was granted under Appendix Innovator, Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules (“the applicant’s current leave”), and<br>(b) the endorsing body or legacy endorsing body from which the approval letter is sought by the applicant endorsed the applicant (pursuant to the immigration rules) on or before 12th April 2023 in connection with the application which gave rise to the grant of the applicant’s ... leave, and<br>(c) where the applicant has been granted leave, or further leave under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules (“the relevant AIF leave”), the applicant has had previous leave granted, before the grant of the relevant AIF leave, under—<br>(i) Appendix Innovator or Appendix Start-up to the immigration rules; or<br>(ii) Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules, in a case where the leave was granted following an endorsement given on or before 12th April 2023.</td><td>Fees 6.2A.A1, 8.2.1</td></tr>
<tr><td>9.26.2</td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application for an approval letter from an endorsing body in respect of a proposed application for limited or indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules where—<br>(a) the applicant has limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom which was granted under Appendix Innovator, Appendix Innovator Founder or Appendix Start-up to the immigration rules (“the applicant’s current leave)”,<br>(b) the endorsing body from which the approval letter is sought by the applicant did not endorse the applicant (pursuant to the immigration rules) in connection with the application which gave rise to the grant of the applicant’s current leave,<br>(c) the letter which endorsed the applicant in connection with the applicant’s current leave (“the original letter”) was issued (pursuant to the immigration rules) by a legacy endorsing body which was at the time when the original letter was issued, approved by the Home Office to endorse individuals in connection with applications under Appendix Innovator to those rules, and<br>(d) the applicant cannot obtain an approval letter from that legacy endorsing body as that body no longer endorses individuals or businesses in connection with applications under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules.</td><td>Fees 6.2A.A1, 8.2.1</td></tr>
<tr><td>9.27</td><td colspan="2">Applications for indefinite leave to remain in the United Kingdom as a bereaved partner under Appendix Bereaved Partner or Appendix Gurkha and Hong Kong military unit veteran discharged before 1 July 1997</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>9.27.1</td><td colspan="2">No fee is payable in respect of an application as a bereaved partner under Appendix Bereaved Partner or Appendix Gurkha and Hong Kong military unit veteran discharged before 1 July 1997 to the immigration rules where, at the time of making the application, the applicant appears to the Secretary of State to be destitute.</td><td>Fee 8.1.1</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

[^c22925341][^c-875c32ec69589c68c1af83aa02335eec][^c-569f2ff77bf852801e480bc5417268d3][^c22925351][^c-bba16427670c7bfcb0dd251b1f58d605][^c-380d45e700d8863564bf70bf9d1242e4][^c-24f7d4c3b08502da7224d2ab68ae69be][^c-9cb341f48dfe8b4cf93cc71c8f3b07ba][^c-034620103d3eb75dab3ad83d2cd0e715][^c-6ee5206a2f8159fd92ff595f9759808f][^c-c7648668addb3c2b306c4664e5817598][^c-7de2d05481783d0524e6809173c61a67][^c-b10e87961955b87f7f4e567411b4bf1c][^c-d6af55d6fac8c4faf4666ec410643700][^M_F_8d3cae2d-44f7-4e66-8032-87dcf4d00f29][^c-d58cc55bc76f9d65d3a7e135b81dcc12][^c-6d05c878b568b5f2b7e184bb7a8b7916][^c-b9a7f872ed1ac4efb0321d8e5492ca9d][^c-4c16984560f8633067e8cc5602df7320][^c-c4a047c681e8e6754c7eded32f5e1b87][^c-04ac801945f8211975e181ca1c241823][^c-0c3e0c8b7699c6f2c9186c35c62d0452][^c-86f031e24f84e6917b01edccecd65c25][^c-c27d25f92f4cc7d09992ca7ebd00b438][^c22925371][^c-77ea25231395e02f6a2b796817ee5c68][^c-e7a19e1aa6733d283ead3587b44d5d7f][^c-e7a19e1aa6733d283ead3587b44d5d7f][^c-d06366d900d60e474ba745025f611b6e][^c-1fc8bc98f51312549b6c6ddb482cc2f5][^c-0bfeb754789a298af38383877d348866][^c-b36235e05bdad61c59308ebadf84cafd][^c-8d4b19fea674a4642eaf4b12a1381e31][^c-c53df1bfae230dff9edb0d12e338fa1e][^c-d9228dcdb85f7c85c8a64a5013c058c9][^c-574cb994920f42aea68ae9a5106151d1][^c-dff0fe36e213f5dbbd9345b39f42ce72][^c-64353b6776791bc8afa42870c59d1f6f][^c-6963cde0d9b87b3ca59da933856180cf][^c-c3cc70e97ee9832988cd7f5b0ba756b9][^c-3bdd2fd7eb6417b1e81f837d669b2e3f][^c-94448acb071f47b49bbf2e44439e71dc][^c-00dee81c80bab2e1fa65dfcbf6fe1208][^c-0303ef61822aa82da60f210b532200cf][^c-c28568b5391e840da31e9d9fdc9a4d6e][^c-3e63fb8c5009cedc1bb35982d37002fb][^c-1a2f17a143024c1432a55d1da5b8ea35]

### 3. Applications by dependants[^c-25c4022e1d74197484c49c4235d4cec9]

(1) The fee for an application for leave to remain in the United Kingdom made by the dependant of a main applicant is—

- (a) where the dependant’s application is not made at the same time as the main applicant’s application, the fee paid by the main applicant under Table 6 or 8 (as the case may be) in respect of their last application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom which was made before the dependant’s application;
- (b) in any other case, the fee specified in Table 6 or 8 (as the case may be) in respect of the main applicant’s application.

(2) Sub-paragraph (1) does not apply in respect of applications for which a fee is specified in—

- (a) 6.2C.1 or 6.2C.2 of Table 6; or
- (b) Table 7.

(3) The fee payable in accordance with sub-paragraph (1) is subject to—

- (a) the exceptions and waivers set out in Table 9 (other than the exception in 9.18 of that Table); and
- (b) paragraph 3A.

### 3A. Reduction of fees for Health and Care Visa applications and related applications by dependants[^c-58d12647111d0917b0bd63fa02cc02bd][^c-36d503148380d5988764d64a7d49acfc]

(1) In this paragraph—

- a “Health and Care Visa application” means an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Skilled Worker to the immigration rules, other than as the dependant partner or dependant child of a Skilled Worker, where the certificate of sponsorship issued , or the approval under Sponsor a Worker given,[^c-4b688309ae6c399c6566a9a1e7982165] in respect of the application confirms the applicant’s eligibility for a Health and Care Visa provided for by the Health and Care Visa Guidance;
- a “relevant dependant’s application” means an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Skilled Worker to the immigration rules as the dependant partner or dependant child of a Skilled Worker where—
  - (a) the certificate of sponsorship issued , or the approval under Sponsor a Worker given, in respect of the application by the main applicant confirms the main applicant’s eligibility for a Health and Care Visa provided for by the Health and Care Visa Guidance, or by the Tier 2 Policy Guidance where the main applicant’s application was made before 9.00 a.m. on 1st December 2020, or
  - (b) the applicant has a sponsor’s letter or email referred to in paragraph 9 of the Health and Care Visa Guidance.

(2) In the case of a Health and Care Visa application, the fee under 6.2.6A, 6.2.6B, 6.2.6C or 6.2.6D of Table 6 payable in respect of an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Skilled Worker to the immigration rules is to be reduced—

- (a) by £619[^c-9259256e07cef911d4b98117a8e55432], where it is the fee specified by 6.2.6A of that Table;
- (b) by £1,237[^c-0f92a0e3f571d625ddf428c698304d9d], where it is the fee specified by 6.2.6B of that Table;
- (c)[^c-3f86c88d47a2b5117b9db648105b906a] by £304[^c-f86a34c0b805aacc73e69acc52ef0f6e], where it is the fee specified by 6.2.6C of that Table;
- (d) by £607[^c-bfbe3bc550fea449c4c02a0c22947111], where it is the fee specified by 6.2.6D of that Table.

(3) In the case of a relevant dependant’s application where the application by the main applicant was made at or after 9.00 a.m. on 1st December 2020, the fee payable by virtue of paragraph 3 in respect of an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Skilled Worker to the immigration rules as the dependant partner or dependant child of a Skilled Worker is to be reduced—

- (a) by £619, where it is the fee specified by 6.2.6A of Table 6;
- (b) by £1,237, where it is the fee specified by 6.2.6B of that Table;
- (c)[^c-bd739535ee2234b8620bd56cdf6536ae] by £304, where it is the fee specified by 6.2.6C of that Table;
- (d) by £607, where it is the fee specified by 6.2.6D of that Table.

(4) In the case of a relevant dependant’s application where the application by the main applicant was made before 9.00 a.m. on 1st December 2020, the fee payable by virtue of paragraph 3 in respect of an application for limited leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix Skilled Worker to the immigration rules as the dependant partner or dependant child of a Skilled Worker is to be reduced—

- (a) by £619, where it is the fee specified by 6.2.7 of Table 6 as it continues to have effect by virtue of regulation 14(6) of the Immigration and Nationality (Replacement of Tier 2 and Fees) (Amendment) (EU Exit) Regulations 2020;
- (b) by £1,237, where it is the fee specified by 6.2.8 of that Table as it continues to have effect by virtue of that regulation;
- (c)[^c-92105a42da188d05d27e79d87ef19695] by £304, where it is the fee specified by 6.2.9 of that Table as it continues to have effect by virtue of that regulation (and as modified by paragraph 4(4) of Schedule 2 to the Immigration and Nationality and Immigration Services Commissioner (Fees) (Amendment) Regulations 2022);
- (d) by £607, where it is the fee specified by 6.2.10 of that Table as it continues to have effect by virtue of that regulation (and as modified by paragraph 4(5) of Schedule 2 to the Immigration and Nationality and Immigration Services Commissioner (Fees) (Amendment) Regulations 2022).

(5) In sub-paragraph (1)—

- (a) the references to “the Health and Care Visa Guidance” are references to the document entitled “Health and Care Visa Guidance” published by the Home Office;
- (b) the reference to “the Tier 2 Policy Guidance” is a reference to the document entitled “Tier 2 of the Points-Based System – Policy Guidance” published by the Home Office.

### 4. Applications made by CESC Nationals[^c-919001f832dabad8bc03e5e650cfe326][^c-36d503148380d5988764d64a7d49acfc]

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

### 5. Variation of an application for leave to remain in the United Kingdom[^c-36d503148380d5988764d64a7d49acfc]

(1) This sub-paragraph applies where—

- (a) an application for leave to remain in the United Kingdom is made in respect of a person on or after the day that these Regulations come into force; but
- (b) before that application (“the first application”)[^c-95f282d3434eed00d8abba652aba021f] is determined a further application is submitted which has the effect of varying it.

(2)[^c-26ebdbee830e9d517b1b271554b4445c] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(3)[^c-55c57842d3f1f21082f66129cd07f524] Where sub-paragraph (1) applies [^c-26fe84aa7d9e245f58e6e99f260cbbe6]... the Secretary of State must refund any fee (or fees) paid in respect of the first application.

## SCHEDULE 3 — DOCUMENTS AND ADMINISTRATION

Regulation 5

### 1. Interpretation

In this Schedule—

- [^c-7e39d8bd483500b2a7a63d94c3e01cb0]...
- “Appendix EU biometric residence card” means a document (within the meaning given by section 126(9) of the Nationality, Immigration and Asylum Act 2002) issued to a person granted leave to remain in the United Kingdom under Appendix EU to the immigration rules on which biometric information is recorded under regulation 6A of the Immigration (Provision of Physical Data) Regulations 2006;[^c-21faca45857b055fdef3bd5d37a323ac]
- “**Assisted Voluntary Return programme**” means any arrangements made by the Secretary of State under section 58 of the 2002 Act;
- “**certificate of travel**” means a travel document issued at the discretion of the Secretary of State to a person who has been granted leave to remain in the United Kingdom and is unable to obtain a passport issued by their national authority;
- “[^c-e8a2121bfff6e388f334bccb4fc1b9fb]**contact point meeting**” means, in relation to an applicant under Appendix Innovator Founder to the immigration rules, a meeting between the applicant and an endorsing body to assess progress against the applicant’s business plan;
- “**convention travel document**” means a travel document issued in accordance with Article 28 of the Convention relating to the Status of Refugees done at Geneva on 28th July 1951 [^c22925391];
- “**Direct Airside Transit Visa**” means a transit visa within the meaning of section 41(2) of the 1999 Act, authorising the holder to remain within an airport, without passing through immigration control, pending departure on another flight from the same airport;
- “**document of identity**” means a travel document issued in the United Kingdom to a person who is not a British citizen which enables the holder to make one journey out of the United Kingdom;
- “[^c-9a7f02f7a3d48881acb828b0bd029771]**endorsing body**” means an organisation which is approved by the Secretary of State to endorse an individual or business in connection with an application under the immigration rules;
- [^c-94c6bf0fa0d1d23e502f6f6831a98221]...
- “[^c-6777ec7b7f41493480c83f65551df86d]**ETA**” has the meaning given by section 11C of the 1971 Act (electronic travel authorisations);
- “**stateless person's travel document**” means a travel document issued in accordance with Article 28 of the Convention relating to the Status of Stateless Persons done at New York on 28th September 1954 [^c22925401];
- “**transfer of conditions**” means an addition to a passport,[^c-ba77453a476c6b09d82ab91da12d776a] or other document issued to an applicant which indicates that a person has been granted leave to enter or leave to remain in the United Kingdom.

### 2. Fees for documents and services[^c-54281a7df3c25180d12881a47d5f3cf6]

(1) Table 10 specifies the amount of the fees for the specified applications, requests and other requirements[^c-cdc7fc46fb7fa1bc3bbb7ff5e45fc03d] for and in connection with documents and services[^c-04c01123e70da569e4aef57ac04eca2f] relating to immigration or nationality.

(2) Table 11 provides for exceptions to the requirement to pay fees specified in Table 10 for—[^c-151a5148b0225458d126236e249d2301]

- (a) applications for travel documents and transfers of conditions,
- (b) processing an application or claim which is later rejected as invalid, and
- (c) contact point meetings.

(3) Table 12 specifies the amount of the fees for the specified applications for biometric immigration documents [^c-32f49127bcf305341ddbe7a1ce00860e]....

(4) Table 13 provides for exceptions to the requirement to pay fees specified in Tables 10 and 12[^c-d210463b8a39c2df7a0d450ecae35345].

(5) Paragraph 3 confers a discretion on the Secretary of State to waive or reduce the specified fee in 12.2.4.

(6)[^c-286d492f621cd6eb6be94cffb7767ce1] Paragraph 4 confers a discretion on the Secretary of State to waive, in specified circumstances, any fee specified in Table 10 or 12.

(7)[^c-e60a0b0abf9bad59425816971031adbc] Paragraph 5 confers a discretion on the Secretary of State to waive part of the specified fee in 10.8.1 in specified circumstances.
